# Decisions

## Decisions

| ID | Content | Approach | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DEC-0495 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0082: Surface classification as primary UI-bearing detection (retired OQ discussion-20260329195516830) | - Decision: Make the explicit surface classification the primary SSOT of UI-bearing detection, and content signals a fallback heuristic - Context: The skill docs say surface classification is primary, but the validator implementation infers from HTML/mermaid/content signals. False positives/negatives and a gap between documentation and implementation arise - Rationale: Surface classification is explicit and predictable. Content signals only assist in ambiguous cases - Rejected: Make content signals the primary detection - DO NOT: Do not make content signals the primary of UI-bearing detection. Temptation: Wanting to make it fully automatic with heuristics - Evidence: SRC-0001 P1-04, SRC-0006 | DONE |
| DEC-0496 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0083: Versioning strategy v1.7.6a + v1.7.7 + v1.7.8 (retired OQ discussion-20260329195516830) | - Decision: The versioning of the remediation is v1.7.6a (hotfix) + v1.7.7 (correction) + v1.7.8 (cleanup) - Context: The repository treats v1.7.6 as completed/released. Reopening it as a pre-release would contradict a version that has already been shared/tagged - Rationale: v1.7.6a + v1.7.7 + v1.7.8 is the cleanest operationally. The rollback boundaries are clear - Rejected: Reopen v1.7.6 as a pre-release - DO NOT: Do not turn an already published version back into a pre-release. Temptation: Wanting to avoid a new version number - Evidence: SRC-0001 Section 6 | DONE |
| DEC-0497 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0084: Default prototyping mode override to standard (qfai_prototyping_mode_switch_ux_proposal.md) | - Decision: Change the default prototyping mode from `low-cost` to `standard`. Mode resolution is decided by a precedence chain (1. CLI --mode, 2. discussion artifact recommended_mode, 3. system default=standard). This overrides DR-0080's "low-cost as default" - Context: qfai_prototyping_mode_switch_ux_proposal.md proposes a hybrid model. The discussion artifact recommends the mode, the CLI can override it, and the system default is standard. The user explicitly approved it (2026-03-30) - Rationale: standard targets customer-presentable quality and fits most use cases. low-cost becomes an explicit opt-in. The precedence chain makes mode resolution deterministic and auditable - Rejected-A: Keep low-cost as the default (keep DR-0080) - DO NOT: Do not put the system default back to low-cost. Temptation: static-first needs no setup and is safer - Rejected-B: Ignore the discussion artifact recommendation and use the CLI only - DO NOT: Do not exclude the discussion artifact recommendation from mode resolution. Temptation: Wanting to keep it simple - Evidence: qfai_prototyping_mode_switch_ux_proposal.md §6, user approval 2026-03-30 | DONE |
| DEC-0498 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0085: Full-harness: entrypoints for both CLI and skill (AD-001/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: Provide both a CLI subcommand and skill guidance as entrypoints for the full-harness premium path - Context: With the CLI alone there is no skill guidance and the barrier to using the premium path is high. With the skill alone, CLI automation is impossible - Rationale: Both entrypoints cover the human-interactive (skill) and CI/automation (CLI) use cases - Rejected: Provide only a CLI subcommand (without skill guidance, the value of the premium path is not conveyed) - DO NOT: Do not provide the premium path without skill guidance. Temptation: Thinking the CLI alone is enough | DONE |
| DEC-0499 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0086: Browser QA MVP: 2 phases, smoke + visual (AD-002/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: Limit the v1.7.8 MVP of browser QA to 2 phases, a smoke phase and a visual phase - Context: The full 4-phase pipeline of browser QA (smoke, interaction, visual, accessibility) exceeds the scope of v1.7.8 - Rationale: Establish an MVP that returns real findings with smoke + visual, and defer interaction and accessibility to later releases - Rejected: Implement all 4 phases of Browser QA in v1.7.8 (including advanced heuristics would exceed the scope) - DO NOT: Do not include advanced heuristics in the scope of v1.7.8. Temptation: Wanting to build the complete QA pipeline at once | DONE |
| DEC-0500 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0087: 4-axis → 3-layer: v1.7.8 warning, v1.8.0 error (AD-003/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: The migration from the 4-axis evaluation model to the 3-layer model is a phased one: warning in v1.7.8 and error in v1.8.0 - Context: The 3-layer model was adopted as the formal architecture in DR-0080, but 4-axis implementations remain in existing projects - Rationale: Providing a migration window avoids breaking existing projects while reliably converging on the 3-layer - Rejected: Make 4-axis an error immediately (introduces a breaking change without a migration window) - DO NOT: Do not introduce a breaking change without a migration window. Temptation: Wanting to eliminate the old format immediately | DONE |
| DEC-0501 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0088: Weak strategy: v1.7.8 warning, v1.8.0 error (AD-004/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: Detection of a weak strategy artifact (an incomplete strategy description) is warning in v1.7.8 and error in v1.8.0 - Context: Even when the quality of a strategy artifact is insufficient, it currently passes. It needs to function as a quality gate - Rationale: A warning-error ratchet pattern raises quality step by step. Raise awareness in v1.7.8 and enforce in v1.8.0 - Rejected: Require anti-preference traceability across the entire flow in v1.7.8 (traceability across the entire flow exceeds the scope) - DO NOT: Do not require traceability across the entire flow in v1.7.8. Temptation: Wanting to achieve complete traceability at once | DONE |
| DEC-0502 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0089: External critique/calibration: publish only docs + entrypoint (AD-005/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: The external critique adapter and calibration pack publish only docs and an entrypoint, and hide the internal implementation - Context: Publishing the detailed implementation of critique/calibration would make the API surface too large and future changes difficult - Rationale: Publishing only the entrypoint secures API stability while keeping the flexibility of the internal implementation - Rejected: Publish everything, including internal APIs (the risk of future breaking changes from a larger API surface) - DO NOT: Do not make the internal critique/calibration API public. Temptation: Wanting to publish all APIs for extensibility | DONE |
| DEC-0503 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0090: When render evidence is unavailable: skipped + reason + alternative (AD-006/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: When render evidence capture is impossible, return a skipped status + reason + alternative suggestion - Context: The behavior needs to be defined for cases where render evidence cannot be obtained, such as no browser installed or a headless environment - Rationale: skipped + reason states the situation explicitly, and the alternative suggestion presents the next action, achieving both fail-open and actionability - Rejected: Stop with an error when render evidence is unavailable (breaks non-visual projects and CI environments) - DO NOT: Do not make the unavailability of render evidence an error. Temptation: Wanting to block because quality cannot be guaranteed without evidence | DONE |
| DEC-0504 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0091: Anti-preference: 3-point traceable, taste → axes → review (AD-007/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: Achieve anti-preference traceability at 3 points: taste interview → scoring axes → review findings - Context: Traceability is needed for how a user's taste preference was reflected in the findings of the final review - Rationale: 3-point traceability makes the chain preference → evaluation → findings clear and makes the basis of subjective judgments verifiable - Rejected: Implement anti-preference traceability across the entire flow in v1.7.8 (across the entire flow exceeds the scope) - DO NOT: Do not require traceability across the entire flow in v1.7.8. Temptation: Wanting to achieve complete traceability at once | DONE |
| DEC-0505 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0092: Master convergence doc: a new steering document (AD-008/retired OQ discussion-20260330035428071) | - Decision: Create a new master convergence steering document that tracks all changes of v1.7.8 Canonical Convergence - Context: The correction-and-convergence release corrects many existing specs/artifacts across the board, so a document that captures the overall picture of the changes is needed - Rationale: A single steering document manages the progress, dependencies and completion state of all changes in one place and prevents oversights in the implementation phase - Rejected: Track only with the existing delta.md (delta.md is an after-the-fact record and is insufficient for planning and progress management) - DO NOT: Do not manage the correction-and-convergence release with delta.md alone. Temptation: Thinking the existing mechanism is enough | DONE |
| DEC-0506 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0093: Treat v1.7.9 as a convergence/correction/integration release (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: v1.7.9 is not a new greenfield design but a convergence/correction/integration release that truthfully connects the existing canonical model to validate/discussion/prototyping/docs - Context: Going back to re-discussing the architecture would prevent completing the correction of CAP-0034..0037 and would continue the gap between the release claim and reality - Rationale: Defining it as a correction release fixes the scope on the convergence of existing capabilities and lets truthfulness come first - Rejected: Treat v1.7.9 as a greenfield redesign - DO NOT: Do not make v1.7.9 the venue of a new architecture redesign. Temptation: Wanting to rebuild the points that bother us all at once | DONE |
| DEC-0507 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0094: Unify canonical validator registration into the production validate path (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: Unify canonical UIX validator registration into the production path of `validateProject()`, leaving no gap between an isolated validator and the real operating path - Context: Even if a validator implementation exists, release truthfulness collapses if the production wiring is on a different path - Rationale: Keeping the validation truth path to one aligns the premises of docs/review/test with the execution results - Rejected: Keep the isolated validator and update only the docs - DO NOT: Do not treat a validator capability whose production wiring is not connected as complete. Temptation: Updating the description is quicker than implementing | DONE |
| DEC-0508 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0095: Discussion completion converges on the taste/trend/3-layer canonical family (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: UI-bearing discussion completion has taste interview, trend scan, 3-layer rubric, strong strategy and strong screen contract as its canonical completion family - Context: Leaving the legacy 4-axis completion in place would split the field family across validator/template/reviewer - Rationale: Fixing the discussion completion model to one lets downstream review/prototyping/validation refer to the same artifact family - Rejected: Let the legacy 4-axis coexist as a canonical default - DO NOT: Do not put the 4-axis legacy back as the canonical default. Temptation: Wanting to avoid changing the existing templates | DONE |
| DEC-0509 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0096: full-harness is a real user-facing explicit path (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: Make `/qfai-prototyping --mode full-harness` an explicit non-default, real user-facing path, and publish the planner/generator/evaluator phases and the evidence/review obligations - Context: A docs-only nominal path does not convey the responsibilities and expectations of the premium mode to users - Rationale: Defining it as an explicit path fixes the boundary with the standard path, the evidence obligation and the reviewability - Migration: The old entry point `/qfai-prototyping-full-harness` and shortcuts such as `.github/skills/qfai-prototyping-full-harness` have been retired. `/qfai-prototyping --mode full-harness` is the only launch path - Rejected: Keep full-harness as a docs-only reference - DO NOT: Do not publish a nominal premium path and treat it as complete. Temptation: Wanting to put out the name before the real wiring | DONE |
| DEC-0510 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0097: Runtime evidence returns explicit skipped/failed and fake success is prohibited (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: When unsupported/unavailable, render evidence and browser QA return an explicit skipped or failed, and never return a fake success - Context: Treating the absent case of a runtime capability as success would mislead review and release notes - Rationale: Honest runtime reporting keeps truthfulness while handling capability gaps fail-open - Rejected: Treat an unsupported runtime capability as success - DO NOT: Do not round an unsupported runtime up to success. Temptation: Wanting to increase PASSes and move the release forward | DONE |
| DEC-0511 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0098: Unify the docs maturity vocabulary as implemented / foundation-only / deferred (retired OQ discussion-20260330153902875) | - Decision: The maturity expressions in steering / changelog / docs / source comments are unified on `implemented`, `foundation-only` and `deferred` as the standard vocabulary - Context: Wavering between completed / foundation / pending creates contradictions in capability claims and makes review judgment difficult - Rationale: Unifying the vocabulary lets reviewers and implementers share the same maturity semantics - Rejected: Allow free-form maturity vocabulary - DO NOT: Do not express capability maturity in free vocabulary. Temptation: Wanting to rephrase it depending on context | DONE |
| DEC-0512 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0099: DR-0099 | - Date: 2026-03-30 - Title: Spec Auto-Discovery Protocol — 4-source integrated diff detection - Status: adopted - Context: The phenomenon of agents stopping as "unable to work" when the spec argument is omitted occurred frequently across all agents. Extend the Preflight Diff Protocol of spec-0011 and adopt a comprehensive solution that includes the TypeScript implementation, validate integration and traceability verification. - Decision: Integrate 4-source diff detection (git diff origin/main + local changes + timestamp + delta.md) + file-level traceability consistency checks into qfai validate - Alternatives rejected: - git diff only: Already rejected in DR-0006 (cannot support no-git environments) - Full semantic analysis: High implementation cost, handled by incremental improvement - Stopping with an error (when the diff is zero): Recurrence of the same stopping problem - Consequence: Newly add the specDiffDetector + traceabilityValidator modules, revise SKILL.md, extend validate | TODO |
| DEC-0513 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0100: DR-0100 | - Date: 2026-03-30 - Title: Traceability verification is a file-level diff check - Status: adopted - Context: In verifying the consistency between BR/AC changes in the spec and the implementation code, full semantic analysis has a high implementation cost - Decision: Implement Phase 1 as a file-level diff check, with a design that can be extended to line-level/semantic analysis in Phase 2 - Alternatives rejected: - Line-level BR/AC reference check: Considered in Phase 2 - Full semantic analysis: Introduced step by step from Phase 2 onward - Consequence: Check for the presence of a file diff based on the mapping of the Traceability Ledger (16_Traceability-ledger.md) --- ## v1.7.11 Decisions (discussion-20260331120000000) | TODO |
| DEC-0514 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0101: DR-0101 | - Date: 2026-03-31 - Title: Old aggregator compatibility wrapper with deprecation - Status: adopted - Context: Choosing how to minimize the impact on existing users when replacing runAllUixValidators() with the canonical entrypoint - Decision: Adopt the compatibility wrapper (option b). Transparently convert old aggregator calls to the new implementation and provide a deprecation window - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Complete removal: A breaking change for existing consumers with no migration path. DO NOT: Do not remove the public interface without a wrapper. Temptation: Simplifying the code by removing everything at once - (c) Side-by-side indefinitely: A permanent dual-maintenance cost. DO NOT: Do not make a dual implementation permanent. Temptation: Keeping both maximizes compatibility - Consequence: Add a wrapper from runAllUixValidators() → runCanonicalUixValidators(). Emit a deprecation warning. Removal is v1.8.0 or later | TODO |
| DEC-0515 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0102: DR-0102 | - Date: 2026-03-31 - Title: 4-axis templates deprecation marking + removal from defaults - Status: adopted - Context: Choosing how to handle the old 4-axis templates in the migration to the 3-layer canonical templates - Decision: Adopt deprecation marking (option b). Attach a deprecated marking to the 4-axis templates and remove them from the defaults. Keep them as reference material - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Immediate deletion: Users midway through migration would lose reference material. DO NOT: Do not delete immediately without marking. Temptation: Wanting to eliminate the old format at once - (c) Keep as-is: New users would receive an outdated model. DO NOT: Do not leave deprecated templates in the defaults. Temptation: Wanting to avoid the risk of change - Consequence: Add 6 new canonical templates. Attach deprecated metadata to the old templates. Update 00_index.md to reference the canonical family | TODO |
| DEC-0516 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0103: DR-0103 | - Date: 2026-03-31 - Title: Remove "requested" status from render evidence vocabulary - Status: adopted - Context: Choosing how to eliminate the ambiguity between intent and execution in the status vocabulary of render evidence - Decision: Remove "requested" and keep only the 3 states captured/skipped/failed (option b) - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Keep "requested": It blurs intent and execution. DO NOT: Do not include intent in the completion status. Temptation: Planning tracking is convenient - (c) Add "pending": It complicates the state machine. DO NOT: Do not add a new status without resolving the ambiguity. Temptation: Wanting to subdivide - Consequence: The evidence report has only captured/skipped/failed. "captured" requires actual execution evidence | TODO |
| DEC-0517 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0104: DR-0104 | - Date: 2026-03-31 - Title: Implement all 4 browser QA phases - Status: adopted - Context: Choosing the scope of the browser QA phase runner (smoke/visual/interaction/accessibility) - Decision: Implement all 4 phases (option a). Partial/foundation-only produces dishonest reporting - Alternatives rejected: - (b) Smoke+visual only: interaction/accessibility stay as stubs. DO NOT: Do not register a phase while it is still a stub. Temptation: Implementing only 2 phases first - (c) Foundation-only: Showing all phases as available gives meaningless results. DO NOT: Do not expose without an implementation. Temptation: Implementing it later - Consequence: The 4-phase runner returns actual analysis results. Honest empty findings are allowed only when truly empty | TODO |
| DEC-0518 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0105: DR-0105 | - Date: 2026-03-31 - Title: Skip v1.7.10 — proceed directly to v1.7.11 - Status: adopted - Context: v1.7.10 is unreleased. Choosing the next release number after v1.7.9 - Decision: Skip v1.7.10 and proceed directly to v1.7.11 (option b) - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Release v1.7.10 retroactively: A version with no substance would get mixed into the timeline. DO NOT: Do not issue an unreleased number retroactively. Temptation: Wanting to keep the numbers consecutive - Consequence: v1.7.11 is positioned as the completion release immediately after v1.7.9 | TODO |
| DEC-0519 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0108: DR-0108 | - Date: 2026-04-01 - Title: Prototyping skill-centered truth (discussion-20260401215536131 D-003) - Status: adopted - Context: spec-0012 records the removal of the CLI command, but \_policies/docs/steering describe the `qfai prototyping` command as current. Unify all layers in v1.7.12 - Decision: Make the `/qfai-prototyping` skill the only truth for users and remove all CLI command references - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Revive the CLI command: Outside the scope of the v1.7.12 correction release. DO NOT: Do not document a command that does not exist. Temptation: CLI command-first is more traditional - Consequence: Remove command references from all of README, steering, policy and spec - Evidence: discussion-20260401215536131 D-003, R-002 | TODO |
| DEC-0520 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0109: DR-0109 | - Date: 2026-04-01 - Title: README command count 6 → 5 (discussion-20260401215536131 D-005) - Status: adopted - Context: README.md/product.md state 6 commands, but the implementation has 5 commands (init, validate, report, doctor, guardrails) - Decision: Correct it to the right command count of 5 - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Revive the prototyping command to make 6: Contradicts DR-0108. DO NOT: Do not include an unimplemented command in the count. Temptation: A bigger number in the documentation makes a better impression - Consequence: Fix docs/steering - Evidence: discussion-20260401215536131 D-005 ### v1.7.13 Canonical Sidecar Convergence (implementation-derived, 2026-04-04) | TODO |
| DEC-0521 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0281: Canonical/Legacy Validator Separation | - Decision: Register only `runCanonicalUixValidators()` in the production-path `validate.ts`, and move the DDP validators to the `legacy/` namespace - Context: The former monolithic `uixValidators.ts` mixed the responsibilities of canonical and legacy - Rationale: Improve the reliability of the production path and separate the legacy migration tooling. Canonical validators emit `category: "canonical"`, which downstream tooling can distinguish - Rejected: Leave the legacy validators in the production path (lowers the detection accuracy of canonical contract violations) - DO NOT: Do not register legacy validators in the validate.ts pipeline. Temptation: Wanting to keep them for backward compatibility | DONE |
| DEC-0522 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0282: prototyping.yaml as Required Side Artifact | - Decision: Add prototyping.yaml as a required artifact of the discussion-pack and make it a blocker of the SDD preflight - Context: The prototyping mode recommendation was not structured in the discussion-pack, so the basis of the mode selection could not be traced - Rationale: prototyping.yaml explicitly captures the basis of mode selection and enables schema validation in the SDD preflight - Rejected: Make prototyping.yaml optional (a missing mode recommendation could not be detected) - DO NOT: Do not make prototyping.yaml optional. Temptation: It looks unnecessary for non-UI projects | SUPERSEDED (by DEC-0743) |
| DEC-0523 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0283: Existence-Based Precedence (D-5) | - Decision: Treat the namespaced contract as authoritative by the mere existence of the `prototyping` key in prototyping.yaml (not by the validity of its value) - Context: In the old implementation, the legacy fallback was triggered by a value validity check, causing unintended mode selection - Rationale: The key existence check prevents the legacy fallback even for a malformed namespaced block and shows an explicit error - Rejected: Precedence based on value validity (the legacy fallback would be triggered silently) - DO NOT: Do not use value-based precedence. Temptation: Wanting to fall back to the default even for an empty prototyping block | DONE |
| DEC-0524 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0284: Add IssueCategory "canonical" | - Decision: Add "canonical" to the IssueCategory type, as the category of issues emitted by the new canonical validators - Context: The two values "compatibility" and "change" could not distinguish a canonical contract violation from a legacy warning - Rationale: Downstream tooling (report, CI checks) can distinguish canonical vs compatibility vs change | DONE |
| DEC-0525 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0285: Report Prototyping Section as Foundation-Only | - Decision: Add a prototyping observability section to report.ts, but do not integrate it into blocking validation in v1.7.13 - Context: Provide it only as observability until the quality of prototyping data stabilizes - Rationale: Introduce it gradually as foundation-only and establish a data model toward future validation integration | DONE |
| DEC-0526 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0286: Harness Loop Status Normalization | - Decision: Normalize the termination status of the harness loop to "converged" / "max-iterations" (replacing the old "accepted" / "cap-reached") - Context: The evidence summary referred to "accepted", but the loop emitted "converged", so the switch case did not match (a bug) - Rationale: Terminology alignment + bug fix | DONE |
| DEC-0527 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0287: ModeGuidance "premium" → "full-harness" | - Decision: Change the mode returned by ModeGuidance.recommend() from "premium" to "full-harness", making it a valid PrototypingMode value - Context: "premium" is not a valid value of PrototypingMode, and a type mismatch occurred silently - Rationale: Type safety + terminology alignment | DONE |
| DEC-0528 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0288: Prototyping Calibration Config Block | - Decision: Add a prototyping.calibration stanza to qfai.config.yaml and set default values (accept: 0.8, refine: 0.5, maxIterations: 15) - Context: The calibration thresholds were hard-coded, so per-project adjustment was not possible - Rationale: Config-driven calibration enables per-project tuning ### v1.7.13 Supplement (derived from commit history analysis, 2026-04-04) | DONE |
| DEC-0529 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0289: Phase1 Ratchet Mechanism | - Decision: When config.uiux.phase1ReleaseDate is set, downgrade UIX-VAL-\* errors within 30 days of the release date to warning - Context: If hard failures occur frequently during the initial rollout period of the canonical UIX validators, the barrier to adoption becomes high - Rationale: A 30-day grace period allows a gradual migration, and full enforcement takes over after the deadline | DONE |
| DEC-0530 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0290: DDH Validator Sidecar Source Mapping | - Decision: Completely rewrite the 7 validators of discussionDesignHardening to a sidecar-first model. Change the read source from the 03_Story-Workshop.md DDS section to the uiux/ sidecar files - Context: The DDS section is monolithic and hard to maintain, whereas sidecar files are modular - Rationale: Having each validator refer to a clear sidecar file as its primary source separates responsibilities and improves maintainability | DONE |
| DEC-0531 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0291: State Coverage Required States Change | - Decision: Change the required states of state coverage from ["empty","loading","error","populated"] to ["default","loading","empty","error"] - Context: "populated" is a subset of "default", and "default" is more accurate as the initial display state - Rationale: "default" means the initial display of a screen, and it is conceptually more correct to treat populated/empty as variants of default | DONE |
| DEC-0532 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0292: Nested Bullet Canonical Format with CSV Fallback | - Decision: Make the nested bullet list the canonical format for the list-type fields of strategy and screen contract, and keep inline CSV as a legacy fallback - Context: The CSV format lacks the expressiveness for complex data structures - Rationale: Nested bullets are better for readability and structure, and the CSV fallback keeps backward compatibility with existing packs | DONE |
| DEC-0533 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0293: QFAI-VIS-002 Severity Downgrade to Info | - Decision: Downgrade QFAI-VIS-002, the absence of an HTML+CSS visual mock, from warning to info - Context: In the sidecar-first model, the HTML mock is an optional fallback and not the primary truth - Rationale: Sidecar artifacts become the primary UI definition, and the absence of an HTML mock is no longer a quality problem | DONE |
| DEC-0534 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0106: DR-0106 | - Date: 2026-04-01 - Title: v1.7.12 4-axis template complete removal (discussion-20260401215536131 D-004) - Status: adopted - Context: DR-0087 adopted a phased migration (v1.7.8 warning → v1.8.0 error), but v1.7.12 decides to completely remove the old 4-axis templates from the init output. Deprecation marking is already complete, so ample migration grace has been secured - Decision: In v1.7.12, completely remove the old 4-axis templates (20_eval_axis_usability, 21_consistency, 22_accessibility, 23_delight) from the active path. Only the new 3-layer family (invariant, trend-derived, product-specific, aggregate, dynamic-overrides) is the default - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Maintain in parallel: The state of one repository with two truths continues. DO NOT: Do not leave the old 4-axis as an active default. Temptation: Wanting to avoid the risk of change - Consequence: Init generation, validators and tests all refer only to the new family - Evidence: discussion-20260401215536131 D-001, D-004, R-001 | TODO |
| DEC-0535 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0107: DR-0107 | - Date: 2026-04-01 - Title: HTML/CSS mock → optional/fallback (discussion-20260401215536131 D-002) - Status: adopted - Context: The AC/TC of spec-0010 list the HTML/CSS mock as a mandatory requirement for UI-bearing completion. In the v1.7.12 canonical truth model, visual layout belongs to the discussion artifacts and is not a completion condition of the spec/policy - Decision: Demote the HTML/CSS mock to optional/fallback. Do not use it as a completion gate in spec/policy/validator - Alternatives rejected: - (a) Delete the HTML/CSS mock entirely: Fallback value remains. DO NOT: Do not delete it entirely (it can be used optionally within a discussion). Temptation: Wanting to erase everything unnecessary - Consequence: AC/TC rewrite, change of validator expectations - Evidence: discussion-20260401215536131 D-002, R-003 | TODO |
| DEC-0536 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0294: Complete removal of the compatibility IssueCategory (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Completely remove "compatibility" from the IssueCategory union type, leaving only "canonical" \| "change" - Context: v1.7.13 introduced the canonical/legacy validator separation, but "compatibility" remained in IssueCategory and its meaning was ambiguous - Rationale: QFAI v1.7.14 is the release that declares a current-only SSOT. Eliminating the concept of legacy/compatibility at the type level and dividing all issues in two, "canonical" (specification conformance) and "change" (change tracking), simplifies the category system of the report output and the validators - Rejected-A: Rename "compatibility" to "legacy" and keep it (legacy itself goes against the current-only SSOT) - DO NOT: Do not reintroduce a category meaning compatibility/legacy/migration into IssueCategory. Temptation: Wanting to keep a category for backward-compatibility checks | DONE |
| DEC-0537 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0295: Canonical Prototyping Surfaces — abolish the -ui suffix (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Change PrototypingSurface from web-ui/mobile-ui/desktop-ui to the 5 values web/mobile/desktop/cli/mixed. "non-ui" is a classification outside the prototyping surfaces - Context: In v1.7.13, surfaces with the -ui suffix such as web-ui existed, and "non-ui" was also included in the prototyping surfaces - Rationale: The -ui suffix is redundant (prototyping itself presupposes UI). Adding cli and explicitly separating non-ui from the prototyping surfaces resolves the confusion between the discussion UI-bearing judgment and the enumeration of prototyping surfaces - Rejected-A: Keep the -ui suffix and add only cli (the inconsistency of suffixes would grow) - DO NOT: Do not bring the -ui suffix back to prototyping surface names. Temptation: For compatibility with existing packs | DONE |
| DEC-0538 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0110: Surface Classification split in two — discussion UI-bearing vs visual/browser evidence (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Split the "UI-bearing" judgment into 2 independent functions: isDiscussionUiBearingPrototypingSurface() (web/mobile/desktop/cli/mixed) and requiresVisualBrowserEvidenceSurface() (web/mobile/desktop/mixed, excluding cli) - Context: In v1.7.13, a single isUiBearingSurface() judged both the discussion pack structure requirements and the prototyping evidence obligations. cli is discussion UI-bearing but does not need browser evidence - Rationale: The cli surface needs UI definition files (sidecars and so on) but does not need visual evidence by screenshot/Playwright. Separating the concerns prevents a cli pack from being wrongly burdened with browser QA obligations - Rejected-A: Classify cli as non-UI-bearing (cli also needs its UI design intent documented) - DO NOT: Do not exclude cli from discussion UI-bearing. Temptation: Thinking cli is a CUI and therefore not UI-bearing | DONE |
| DEC-0539 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0111: Strict Classification Validation — contradiction-detection gate (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Newly add readValidatedClassificationBlock() and make semantic contradictions inside the classification block (ui_bearing=false + non-empty secondary_surfaces, ui_bearing=true + primary_surface=non-ui and so on) a hard error - Context: In v1.7.13, readClassificationBlock() only returned the parse result, and each validator performed the contradiction checks individually - Rationale: Eliminate the risk of production paths such as execution.ts continuing to operate on an invalid classification. Consolidate contradiction detection into a common gate in the parser layer so that all consumers receive consistent strict validation - Rejected-A: Keep the individual validator checks (duplicate implementation and the risk of inconsistency) - DO NOT: Do not use readClassificationBlock() (non-strict) in the execution path. Temptation: There may be cases where strict validation is too heavy | DONE |
| DEC-0540 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0112: Namespaced-Only Schema — legacy top-level keys hard-reject (v1.7.14) | - Decision: If legacy top-level recommendation keys of prototyping.yaml (recommended_mode, allowed_modes and so on) exist, it is a hard error regardless of whether a namespaced `prototyping:` block exists - Context: In the existence-based precedence of v1.7.13 (DR-0283), the namespaced block took priority but legacy keys were tolerated as a warning (QFAI-PROT-231/232) - Rationale: As a current-only SSOT release, structurally prohibit the very existence of the legacy schema. The warning→error promotion clearly ends the migration period - Rejected-A: Keep the warning and promote to error in v1.8.0 (extending the migration delays convergence) - DO NOT: Do not tolerate the existence of legacy top-level keys with a warning. Temptation: Wanting to soften the impact on existing projects | DONE |
| DEC-0541 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0113: Semantic Invariant SSOT — recommended_mode ∈ allowed_modes (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Newly add validateRecommendationSemantics() in recommendationSemantics.ts and make it the SSOT shared across all layers, validator/runtime/execution/CLI, for the requirement that recommended_mode is included in allowed_modes (QFAI-PROT-154) - Context: In v1.7.13, the semantic mismatch check existed only in the validator (prototypingRecommendation.ts), and the execution/CLI paths were unverified - Rationale: A gap in verifying a semantic invariant leads directly to a runtime error. Make the shared helper the single source of truth, and have all layers, the parser (extractRecommendation), resolver, execution, CLI, validator and preflight, refer to the same logic - Rejected-A: Add individual checks to execution.ts (duplicate implementation, risk of inconsistency) - DO NOT: Do not implement the semantic invariant check anywhere other than the helper. Temptation: Writing it inline in each layer is quicker | DONE |
| DEC-0542 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0114: Canonical Strategy Decision Vocabulary (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Introduce a canonical enum (template, component-library, design-system, native-pattern, bespoke, none) for the strategy decision fields (decision, chosen_option, candidate_options) and prohibit free-form text - Context: In v1.7.13, the strategy validator did only structural checks on 8 fields, and the value of decision was an arbitrary string - Rationale: A canonical vocabulary enables comparison, aggregation and automated analysis of strategy decisions. Enforcing the state machine selection_required=true → ≥2 candidates + non-"none" decision, and selection_required=false → decision="none", guarantees semantic consistency - Rejected-A: Allow free-form and make the enum only a recommendation (comparison and aggregation would be difficult) - DO NOT: Do not allow values outside the canonical enum in the strategy decision fields. Temptation: Wanting to enter project-specific options freely | DONE |
| DEC-0543 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0115: Current-Only SSOT — complete removal of migration/defer wording (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Completely remove wording such as v2.0 defer, legacy deprecation, reconsidered-in-v2.0 and migration guide from steering documents such as product.md and manifest.md - Context: The steering docs up to v1.7.13 had remnants of the migration period, such as "reconsider in v2.0" and "legacy is deprecated" - Rationale: v1.7.14 is a current-only SSOT release. Shipped documents describe only the current specification and exclude mentions of future migration or past compatibility. This eliminates the risk of document readers misunderstanding the migration status - Rejected-A: Keep the migration history as an appendix (goes against the current-only principle) - DO NOT: Do not reintroduce migration/defer/reconsidered wording into steering/product/manifest. Temptation: Wanting to keep the history | DONE |
| DEC-0544 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0116: Independent Evaluator Panel 3-Layer Structure (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Introduce a 3-layer independent evaluation panel into the iterative improvement loop of full-harness mode. L1: product-surface-reviewer (UI/UX design quality), L2: product-experience-architect (product experience), L3: qa-gatekeeper (process audit) - Context: Two incident reports confirmed a self-evaluation bias in which the generator evaluates itself and over-reports quality scores. Cases occurred where scoringTrace scores were judged converged without improvement, and where the invocation of an independent reviewer was faked with fabricated names - Rationale: Launch L1/L2 in separate contexts with the task tool's background mode and exclude the improvement history, previous scores and generator plan from their input, structurally eliminating bias. weightedTotal is the minimum of L1/L2, so a high score from only one does not lead to accept. product-experience-architect is kind: worker, so it is placed in the evidence phase of agent-routing.yml rather than in review-profiles.yml - Rejected-A: Evaluation by a single reviewer (multi-perspective evaluation is impossible and blind spots remain) - DO NOT: Do not run the iteration evaluation of full-harness with a single agent. Temptation: Thinking one reviewer is enough - Rejected-B: Register product-experience-architect in always_required of review-profiles.yml (the QFAI-AGENT-010 validator rejects it because it is kind: worker) - DO NOT: Do not register a kind: worker agent in review-profiles.yml. Temptation: Wanting to manage all evaluators uniformly in the review profile | DONE |
| DEC-0545 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0117: Score Scope Separation — Discussion ≠ Prototyping (v1.7.14) | - Decision: Clearly separate the discussion 3-layer aggregate scores (design direction quality) from the prototyping scoringTrace (implementation fidelity), and prohibit copying - Context: An incident report confirmed a case where the aggregate scores at discussion completion were copied as-is into the prototyping scoringTrace, and no independent evaluation of implementation quality was performed. As a result, all iterations were falsely judged converged with the same score - Rationale: Discussion scores measure "which design direction best satisfies the evaluation criteria" (what), and prototyping scores measure "how good the implementation quality is against the chosen anchor" (how well). The evaluation targets differ fundamentally, so reusing scores is semantically invalid - Rejected-A: Use the discussion scores as the initial values of prototyping (because the evaluation targets differ, they are inappropriate even as initial values) - DO NOT: Do not use the discussion aggregate scores as initial or reference values of the prototyping scoringTrace. Temptation: Wanting prototyping to start from a high score if discussion scored high | DONE |
| DEC-0546 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0200: converged requires iterationCount>=2 (v1.7.15) | - Decision: The converged judgment requires iterationCount >= 2, and plateauLookback >= 2 is enforced in the CalibrationLoader schema - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0547 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0201: Reviewer placeholder reject list frozen (v1.7.15) | - Decision: Freeze the reviewer placeholder reject list at the 6 values "qfai", "default", "auto", "system", "unknown" and "". The resolvedReviewer ?? "qfai" fallback is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0548 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0202: commitSha mandatory in full-harness (v1.7.15) | - Decision: commitSha is mandatory when running full-harness, and it is a runtime error when it cannot be obtained - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0549 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0203: packVersion from pack metadata only (v1.7.15) | - Decision: packVersion is obtained dynamically from the pack metadata via CalibrationLoader. Hard-coding is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0550 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0204: weightedTotal = min(l1.total, l2.total) always (v1.7.15) | - Decision: computeWeightedTotal always returns Math.min(l1.total, l2.total) - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0551 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0205: specCoverage from real diffs only (v1.7.15) | - Decision: specCoverage is derived from real spec/runtime evidence. Zero-seeded output is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0552 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0206: uiFidelity observation-only (v1.7.15) | - Decision: uiFidelity is composed only from DOM parse / browser QA / render evidence. A synthetic mockPaths pass is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0553 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0207: CalibrationLoader wired in execution.ts (v1.7.15) | - Decision: CalibrationLoader is called in execution.ts after loadConfig() (not in config.ts) - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0554 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0208: Fail-fast no silent fallback (v1.7.15) | - Decision: When required evidence is missing, fail immediately with a runtime error. Filling in default values is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0555 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0209: Pre-scored l1/l2 path elimination (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: Remove l1/l2 from the request type of runFullHarness(). Scoring is executed centrally inside the runtime. Throw immediately when panelInputs is missing - Context: There was a path where request.l1/l2 accepted values pre-scored externally and the runtime skipped scoring. As a result, scores that are not evidence-grounded were used for the converged judgment - Rationale: Confining the only path of scoring inside the runtime structurally prevents deviation from evidence - Rejected: Keep request.l1/l2 as optional (it becomes a breeding ground for silent bypass) - DO NOT: Do not leave a pre-scored path in the request type. Temptation: Wanting to make it optional for backward compatibility - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0556 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0210: l2Evidence.ts new file for real artifact derivation (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: Newly add l2Evidence.ts in packages/qfai/src/core/prototyping/. Derive the L2 input from real artifacts with buildDiscussionAxisInputs / buildScreenContractInputs / buildTrendAlignmentInputs - Context: An L2 dummy object (aggregateScore:0, evidenceRefs:[]) was generated inline in execution.ts - Rationale: Consolidate the reading of real artifacts into a dedicated module and prevent the dummy object from recurring. Throw when evidence is insufficient - Rejected: Add the build functions inline in execution.ts (blurs the module's responsibility) - DO NOT: Do not embed the L2 evidence derivation in execution.ts. Temptation: Wanting to finish in one file - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0557 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0211: CalibrationLoader fail-open removal (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: Remove all fail-open paths of CalibrationLoader. Abolish the DEFAULT_PACK fallback / filling in version="1.0.0" / injecting default values when thresholds are missing - Context: Because it could continue with DEFAULT_PACK even when the pack was absent, a full-harness run without calibration was possible - Rationale: A fail-closed design structurally guarantees the existence and validity of the calibration pack - Rejected: Keep DEFAULT_PACK as a safe default only when the pack is absent (it would become the last bastion of fail-open) - DO NOT: Do not leave a fallback in CalibrationLoader. Temptation: Wanting to reduce friction in the first-time setup - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0558 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0212: TerminationContext receives CalibrationPack only (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: computeTerminationReason() / computeStatus() in history.ts receive { calibration: CalibrationPack; history: FullHarnessHistory }. Resolving plateauLookback from anywhere other than the pack is abolished - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0559 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0213: Screen-level UiObservation with ScreenObservation type (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: Rebuild UiObservationSummary based on ScreenObservation[]. Abolish the flatten aggregation and keep observation results at the screen level - Context: Because the DOM labels / actions / mockPaths of all screens were flattened and aggregated, per-screen quality judgment was impossible - Rationale: Keeping them at the screen level makes it possible to detect insufficient-evidence for individual screens. uiFidelityBuilder also builds observed per screen - Rejected: Keep the flatten aggregation and add a screen breakdown inside the summary (a double structure) - DO NOT: Do not let the flatten aggregation and the screen level coexist. Temptation: Wanting to keep both for backward compatibility - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0560 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0214: bundleWriter schema v2 only (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: bundleWriter outputs only schema v2 (8-category evidenceRefs + the new FullHarnessIteration type). Coexistence of v1/v2 is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0561 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0215: Validator 14-rule error upgrade (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: Promote the 14 items of prototypingEvidence.ts from warning to error. Semantic changes that are new are split off into new rule IDs - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0562 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0216: DB coverage binary policy (v1.7.15 rev2) | - Decision: When there are declared DB objects but no observation, it is a full-harness failure. "Continue while always treating as missing" is prohibited - Status: Adopted | TODO |
| DEC-0563 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0217: cli/full-harness reject at 4 layers (v1.7.15 rev4) | - Decision: Reject the combination of `cli` + `full-harness` at 4 layers: the CLI / derivePrototypingObligations / runFullHarness / the validator - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Running full-harness on a non-UI surface only runs a meaningless Browser QA cycle. Extended from 3 layers (rev2 DR-0210) to 4 layers - Alternatives: (A) Reject at the CLI layer only — insufficient, can be bypassed / (B) 4-layer defense (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, WS-1 | TODO |
| DEC-0564 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0218: screen contract-based Browser QA targets (v1.7.15 rev4) | - Decision: Change the Browser QA targets from the fixed value `"/primary"` to dynamic derivation based on the screen definitions in `40_screen_contracts.md` - Status: Adopted - Rationale: A fixed value caused missed measurements of multiple screens, which an audit detected as a discrepancy - Alternatives: (A) Keep "/primary" — missed measurements / (B) Screen contract derivation (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, WS-2 | TODO |
| DEC-0565 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0219: Browser QA evidence chain hard-fail on empty (v1.7.15 rev4) | - Decision: When `iterations[].evidenceRefs.browserQa` is empty, it is a hard fail and a silent pass is prohibited - Status: Adopted - Rationale: An interrupted evidence chain means audit trails cannot be followed. Part of the fail-closed policy - Alternatives: (A) Continue even when empty — breaks the audit chain / (B) Hard fail (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, WS-3 | TODO |
| DEC-0566 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0220: canonical route semantics for runtimeGate/specCoverage (v1.7.15 rev4) | - Decision: In `runtimeGateBuilder.ts` / `specCoverage.ts`, compare by canonical path (the normalized path) and not by URL - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Eliminate the bug in which a URL containing query parameters or a fragment was mistakenly judged to be a different route - Alternatives: (A) Use the URL as is — misjudgment / (B) Canonical path comparison (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, WS-4 | TODO |
| DEC-0567 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0221: L2 structured parse priority over heuristic (v1.7.15 rev4) | - Decision: In L2 evidence collection, prioritize structured parsing (the 20-23 series, 04_Sources.md, 40_screen_contracts.md), and allow the heuristic fallback only when no structured source exists - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Relying on heuristics lowers evidence accuracy and reproducibility - Alternatives: (A) Heuristic first — lower accuracy / (B) Structured parse first (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, WS-5 | TODO |
| DEC-0568 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0222: parameterized route pattern-based matching (v1.7.15 rev4, retired OQ resolution) | - Decision: Adopt pattern-based matching (Option B) for matching parameterized Browser QA routes (e.g., `/orders/:id`) - Status: Adopted - Rationale: With exact match only, the Browser QA evidence chain for dynamic routes breaks. Canonical normalization is over-engineering - Alternatives: (A) Exact match only — no support for dynamic routes / (B) Pattern-based matching (adopted) / (C) Canonical normalization — excessive complexity - Source: discussion-20260414195449523, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0569 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0223: ui[] row validation inline in prototypingEvidence.ts (v1.7.15 rev9, retired OQ resolution) | - Decision: Implement the validation of the 3 row-level fields of `runtimeGate.ui[]` (declaredRef/renderEvidenceRefs[]/browserQaEvidenceRefs[]) inline within `prototypingEvidence.ts` (Option A) - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Design doc §6-1-2 names `prototypingEvidence.ts` as a changed file. Extracting a small, cohesive validation unit into a separate module has no architectural benefit. Inline preserves cohesion - Alternatives: (A) inline in prototypingEvidence.ts (adopted) / (B) extract validateRuntimeGateUiRow() to separate utility — unnecessary module split - Source: discussion-20260416092414328, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0570 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0224: browserQaEvidenceRefs[] always required non-empty (v1.7.15 rev9, retired OQ resolution) | - Decision: `runtimeGate.ui[].browserQaEvidenceRefs[]` is always required non-empty even when "browser QA was not performed", and an empty array is a hard fail (Option A) - Status: Adopted - Rationale: The fail-closed policy of design doc §3-2. Consistent with the precedent of rejecting an empty `runtimeGate.evidenceRefs` array in the rev8 retired OQ. Allowing empty creates a loophole where the builder outputs empty and the validator passes it - Alternatives: (A) always required non-empty (adopted) / (B) allow empty when no browser QA run — violates fail-closed - Source: discussion-20260416092414328, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0571 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0225: per-axis evidenceRefs validation granularity (v1.7.15 rev9, retired OQ resolution) | - Decision: Validate `fullHarness.iterations[].l1/l2.axes[].evidenceRefs[]` at per-axis granularity (Option A). If any axis has an empty array, it is a validator error - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Conforms to the per-element description of design doc §6-1-3. Allowing aggregate leniency (an error only when all axes are empty) would let some axes pass without evidence - Alternatives: (A) per-axis validation (adopted) / (B) aggregate leniency — breaks the per-axis traceability contract - Source: discussion-20260416092414328, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0572 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0226: full README enumeration of all concrete-ref leaf fields (v1.7.15 rev9, retired OQ resolution) | - Decision: State all leaf fields of the concrete-ref contract in the README (ui[].declaredRef, ui[].renderEvidenceRefs[], ui[].browserQaEvidenceRefs[], axes[].evidenceRefs[], reviewerLogs[].evidenceRefs[]) (Option A) - Status: Adopted - Rationale: DoD §5-6 "make docs/validator partial-strictness mismatches zero" is a hard gate. Design doc §9 explicitly prohibits "weakening the README's wording to make them agree" - Alternatives: (A) full enumeration (adopted) / (B) minimal note — violates DoD §5-6 - Source: discussion-20260416092414328, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0573 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0227: UI-bearing discussion requires design guideline research (v1.7.17) | - Decision: In a UI-bearing discussion, design guideline research is a mandatory step in addition to Trend Scan. Include at least 1 line of references suited to the project context, such as Material Design / WCAG / Apple HIG / platform/library guidelines - Status: Adopted - Rationale: The root cause of visual quality failures is missing upstream research, not prototyping, so it must be closed in discussion - Alternatives: (A) Correct after the fact in the prototyping skill — cannot close the root cause / (B) Make it a discussion mandatory step (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260418170937652, DR-001 | TODO |
| DEC-0574 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0228: `design_guideline_research` becomes canonical Trend Scan category (v1.7.17) | - Decision: Add `design_guideline_research` to the canonical categories of `04_Sources.md` - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Putting guideline evidence on the same traceability surface as the existing source registry lets the validator and downstream specs refer to the same SSOT - Alternatives: (A) Create a separate file — category drift / (B) Integrate into 04_Sources (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260418170937652, DR-002 | TODO |
| DEC-0575 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0229: Trend-derived `score_anchors` require quantitative proxy and warning-first rollout (v1.7.17) | - Decision: The `score_anchors` of the legacy trend-derived scoring artifact prohibit abstract adjectives alone and must include at least one quantitative proxy such as a px value / ratio / rule ID / class name / library default. The validator severity is warning in v1.7.17 - Status: Adopted - Rationale: An anchor without concreteness lets a low-quality UI through even with a high score. On the other hand, a staged rollout is appropriate given the impact on existing packs - Alternatives: (A) Immediate error — migration shock / (B) warning-first ratchet (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260418170937652, DR-003 / retired OQ resolution | TODO |
| DEC-0576 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0230: Validator ownership split = trendScan coverage + scoringReady concreteness (v1.7.17) | - Decision: The guideline coverage validator extends `packages/qfai/src/core/validators/uix/trendScan.ts`, and the anchor concreteness validator extends `packages/qfai/src/core/validators/uix/scoringReady.ts`. No new top-level validator is added - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Category completeness is the responsibility of the sources schema and anchor concreteness that of the scoring-ready schema, which matches the current canonical validator boundaries - Alternatives: (A) A new top-level validator — blurs the ownership split / (B) Extend the existing uix modules (adopted) - Source: discussion-20260418170937652, DR-004 / retired OQ resolution | TODO |
| DEC-0577 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0231: Make Brave Search MCP the primary search provider (v1.8.0) | - Decision: Adopt Brave Search as the primary search MCP of Web Research Enhancement - Status: Adopted - Rationale: It has the best balance of community adoption, documentation and ease of integration with CLI agents - Alternatives: (A) Make Tavily primary / (B) Make multiple providers primary on an equal footing - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0578 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0232: Document both the hosted and local npx modes of Firecrawl, and recommend local for sensitive environments (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The Firecrawl integration provides both a hosted URL and a local `npx` configuration, and recommends local for sensitive environments - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Both security and ease of adoption are needed, and fixing a single mode does not meet operational requirements - Alternatives: (A) hosted only / (B) local only - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0579 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0233: SSE transport is out of scope for v1.8.0 and deferred to post-v1.8.0 (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The MCP transports in scope are only stdio and HTTP, and SSE-based integration is not handled in v1.8.0 - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Given the Apify SSE deprecation, formally adopting SSE at this point carries high maintenance cost and uncertainty - Alternatives: (A) Include SSE in v1.8.0 / (B) Leave it ambiguous without a per-transport policy - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0580 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0234: Fix content sanitization at moderate scope (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The sanitizer is limited to removing control chars, `aria-hidden` and `display:none`, and does no ML-based judgment or excessive removal - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Moderate scope is the best balance between security measures and content fidelity - Alternatives: (A) basic only / (B) aggressive semantic sanitization - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0581 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0235: The evaluation harness stays at metrics definitions and is not fixed to a specific tool (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The common specification for evaluation goes only as far as defining the metrics of citation precision, coverage, freshness and security hygiene, and is not fixed to a specific vendor/evaluation tool - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Keep the evaluation foundation replaceable while sharing only the viewpoint of the quality gate - Alternatives: (A) Make a specific evaluation tool mandatory / (B) Do not define the metrics themselves - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0582 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0236: Research sub-agents adopt conservative defaults (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The defaults of the research sub-agents are `max_threads=2` and `max_depth=2`, and higher values are an explicit opt-in - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Prevent runaway parallelism and search depth, and keep a balance among rate limits, cost and traceability - Alternatives: (A) No limit / (B) Fixed at 1 - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0583 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0237: The cache staleness threshold defaults to 24 hours and can be overridden by config (v1.8.0) | - Decision: The default stale threshold of the research cache is 24 hours and can be overridden in settings - Status: Adopted - Rationale: It is a practical balance between freshness and API cost, and differences between use cases can be absorbed by config - Alternatives: (A) A fixed value with no override / (B) Disable the cache - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0584 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0238: The HITL gate fires based on risk (v1.8.0) | - Decision: HITL fires only for high-risk, low-confidence cases, and an excessive approval flow such as per-fetch approval is not adopted - Status: Adopted - Rationale: Concentrate human approval on the situations that need it and do not impair the throughput of normal cases - Alternatives: (A) Require approval for every fetch / (B) No HITL - Source: discussion-20260328212829687, retired OQ | TODO |
| DEC-0585 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0239: Skill-First Prototyping Posture (2026-04-22) | - Decision: active prototyping contract is `/qfai-prototyping [--auto]` plus `qfai validate` / `/qfai-verify` - Decision: declared screen evidence requires both screenshot and HTML snapshot - Decision: legacy full-harness / mode / runtime terminology remains historical or validator-slice vocabulary only - Rationale: simplify user-facing execution while preserving deterministic evidence integrity checks | DONE |
| DEC-0586 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0240: Discussion / Preflight Side Artifact Neutrality (2026-04-22) | - Decision: Discussion-pack readiness and the SDD preflight make the required markdown the main gate, and do not treat `prototyping.yaml` as a mandatory side artifact - Decision: Even if `missingSideArtifacts` remains as a compatibility-shaped field, the current contract does not use it as a readiness blocker - Rationale: To sync with the current behavior of `packages/qfai/src/core/discussionPack.ts` and `packages/qfai/src/core/preflight/sddPreflight.ts` | DONE |
| DEC-0587 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0241: Reviewer-Score Snapshot Evidence Model (2026-04-22) | - Decision: The active evidence schema of full-harness is `reviewerScores[]`, `allReviewerAxesPerfect100`, the snapshot-based `scoringTrace[]` and `iterationBudget` - Decision: `weightedTotal` / `deltaFromPrevious` are historical wording and are not used in the current convergence contract - Rationale: To sync with the current implementation of `packages/qfai/src/core/harness/history.ts` / `resultWriter.ts` / `types.ts` | DONE |
| DEC-0588 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0250: DR-0250..0260: CHG-003 — Assistant-layer Recut + Work-log Surface (2026-05-22) | Source: discussion-20260522081618995 retired OQ (10 resolved, 2 deferred). #### DR-0250: Promotion target is the per-spec `07_Decisions.md` alone (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: The promote target of a `kind: decision` work-log entry is only the per-spec `07_Decisions.md`. A project-level MADR `decisions/` directory is not adopted. - Rationale: Keep a single SSOT per spec. Do not add a parallel decision register. - Rejected option: Creating a project-level MADR `decisions/` (because it adds a parallel SSOT). - Source: user via AskUserQuestion 2026-05-22. #### DR-0251: Adopt the 4-conceptual-layer + Process partition (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: Re-divide into the 6 entries `.qfai/assistant/{constitution,manifest,catalog,process,agents,skills}/`. `steering/` is newly created at the project root and serves as the work-log surface. - Rationale: The industry convergence of Spec Kit (Constitution) + Kiro (Manifest/steering) + Cline (scratchpad) + AAIF. The bulk-rename option (current steering → manifest) is rejected because it would mis-classify 8 of the 12 files. - Rejected options: (B) A 2-directory bulk rename into instructions/ + manifest/; (C) A flat assistant/ with no layers. - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0011W/0012W/0013W. #### DR-0252: Work-log scope is project-level alone (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: The work-log surface is the project-root `.qfai/steering/` alone. Per-spec scope is expressed with the frontmatter `scope: spec-NNNN`. - Rationale: Avoid per-spec path proliferation. Cross-spec memos can also be expressed in frontmatter. - Rejected option: Also providing a per-spec `.qfai/specs/spec-NNNN/steering/` (because of path proliferation). - Source: user via AskUserQuestion 2026-05-22. #### DR-0253: Relocate `spec_required_files.json` to catalog/ (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: Move the existing `.qfai/assistant/manifest/spec_required_files.json` to `.qfai/assistant/catalog/`. The new Manifest layer is dedicated to the project spine. - Rationale: A registry artifact is the Catalog's responsibility and is separated from the Manifest (project spine). - Rejected option: Leave it in the old `manifest/` (conflicts with the meaning of the new Manifest). - Source: user via AskUserQuestion 2026-05-22. #### DR-0254: `.qfai/steering/` is excluded by default in `.gitignore` (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: The work-log surface is untracked by default. A project that opts in can commit it with a `.gitignore` override. - Rationale: Defense-in-depth against accidental secret commits. Teams that want a shared resume context can opt in. - Rejected option: Tracked by default (risk of secret leaks). - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0001. #### DR-0255: The deprecation window for the old paths is 1 minor release (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: The validator accepts the old layout and the new layout in parallel for 1 minor release and emits a `D-DEPRECATED-PATH` warning. In the next minor, acceptance of the old paths is removed and the warning escalates to an error. - Rationale: Consistent with the past ATDD migration cadence (`process/migrations/v1.4.27-atdd-alignment.md`). - Rejected option: A hard cut (removing acceptance of the old paths within 1 release; a large break for consumers). - Source: requirements-analyst, prior migration memo. #### DR-0256: Work-log entries use YAML frontmatter (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: The metadata of `.qfai/steering/*.md` is YAML frontmatter. - Rationale: Consistent with the existing `qfai-*` SKILL.md convention. It also pairs well with prose fields such as `closure-rationale`. - Rejected option: TOML (thin tool ecosystem), JSON (needs separation from the prose body). - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0009. #### DR-0257: Entry filename = `<id>.md`, where `id` is kebab-case ASCII (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: Make the filename stem match the frontmatter `id`. - Rationale: Chronological order in `ls`, unique enough, human-readable. - Rejected option: UUID filenames (low human readability), `YYYY-MM-DD/<slug>.md` (more per-day directories). - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0001. #### DR-0258: Reviewer drift findings are severity error + mandatory justification (advisory-failing) (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: `R-WORKLOG-DRIFT` / `R-REJECTED-READOPT` are severity error but require a non-empty `justification:` field. `qfai validate` rejects an `R-*` finding that lacks a justification. - Rationale: Making natural-language heuristic drift detection a hard block would destroy trust through false positives. Making it warning-only would get it ignored. Advisory-failing takes the middle path. - Rejected option: warning-only (ignored); hard-block (trust destroyed by false positives). - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0001. #### DR-0259: `D-DEPRECATED-PATH` is a warning during the window / an error from sunset (retired OQ resolved) - Decision: A warning during the window, and it escalates to an error at the sunset version. The warning text names the sunset version every time (NFR-0003). - Rationale: An error on day 1 bricks installed copies. Warning-only is ignored. A version-aware phased migration is safe. - Rejected option: An error on day 1 (consumers' installed copies would stop working). - Source: requirements-analyst, SRC-0001. #### DR-0260: AGENTS.md alignment and auto-archival are deferred (retired OQ + retired OQ deferred) - Decision: The AGENTS.md / `CLAUDE.md` symlink discussion is split off into a separate `/qfai-discussion` invocation (target 2026-09-30). Automatic archival of `.qfai/steering/` is substituted by the `W-WORKLOG-STALE` surface of `qfai validate` until the post-v1 dogfooding review (target 2026-12-31). - Rationale: The two have independent discovery scopes. Bundling them into this pack would exceed atomicity. - Source: requirements-analyst, the deferred row in `13_Deferred.md`. | DONE |
| DEC-0589 | .qfai/spec/_policies/08_Decisions.md#DR-0001-0001: DR-0001-0001..0009: Prototyping defect-remediation pack — deferred OQ resolutions (2026-05-24) | Source: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (9 deferred → resolved at `/qfai-sdd` Phase 0). These rows resolve the 9 OQ rows the requirements-analyst left `deferred` for the architect to pick. The acceptance signals for the underlying REQs (REQ-0101..0125) are invariant across each option set; the choices below pin the specific implementation contract for spec-0012 (and any co-shipped spec slices) so downstream implementation, validation, and reviewer-gate code can be written against a single resolution. #### DR-0001-0001: Tailwind ↔ gate hybrid — β whitelist preflight + γ `<body>`-scope (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: Resolve the Tailwind CDN ↔ `findDesignMdViolations` conflict by combining option β (whitelist Tailwind preflight literals in scanner) AND option γ (scope the gate to `<body>` only, skipping the preflight `<style>` block) in a single hybrid remedy. The whitelist enumerates the 5 sentinel literals named in SRC-0001 §B-4 / SRC-0004 §3: `#fff`, `#9ca3af`, `#e5e7eb`, `rgb(59 130 246 / 0.5)`, `--tw-ring-*`. The `<body>`-scope rule excludes `<head>` / preflight `<style>` blocks from scanner input. - Chosen option: β + γ hybrid. - Rejected option α — drop Tailwind CDN: breaks the rapid-prototyping reach the skill depends on; downstream LLMs would lose JIT utility coverage. - DO NOT: drop CDN-based Tailwind from `generator-prompt.md`. Temptation: gate becomes trivial if we ship no CDN. - Rejected option δ — read `tailwind.config.theme.extend` to compute per-iter allowlist: pushes compile-cost into the scanner and creates a new SSOT-sync surface (`tailwind.config` ↔ scanner) without removing the preflight problem. - DO NOT: parse tailwind.config inside the scanner. Temptation: feels like the "correct" semantic remedy. - Rejected option ε — mandate arbitrary-value form everywhere (`rounded-[0.5rem]`): forces authors to abandon utility shorthands for no scanner-side benefit (the preflight literals still leak). - DO NOT: ban utility shorthands. Temptation: feels safer because every value is explicit. - Source REQ: REQ-0101, REQ-0102, REQ-0125. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). #### DR-0001-0002: `--*-shadow*:` strip — option B (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: `SHADOW_DECL_STRIP_RE` extends to **any custom-property declaration whose name contains `shadow`** (regex `--[A-Za-z0-9-]*shadow[A-Za-z0-9-]*:`), not the narrow `--shadow-*:` form. This catches Tailwind v4 emissions such as `--ring-shadow`, `--default-shadow`, `--inset-shadow-*` that the narrow form would miss. - Chosen option: B (`--*-shadow*:`). - Rejected option A — narrow `--shadow-*:` only: misses Tailwind v4 `--ring-shadow`, `--default-shadow` and any future `--<feature>-shadow-*` emissions; would require touching the regex every Tailwind minor. - DO NOT: hard-code `--shadow-` prefix. Temptation: narrowest pattern minimizes false-strip risk. - Rejected option C — parse-as-composite (semantic strip of any `--*` whose value parses as a box-shadow composite): too permissive; will silently strip legitimate color tokens whose computed value happens to look shadow-shaped (e.g. tokens used in both shadow and border contexts), producing false negatives in `scanColors`. - DO NOT: introduce semantic parsing in a regex-stage filter. Temptation: feels future-proof. - Source REQ: REQ-0105. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). #### DR-0001-0003: CJK-aware `countWords` — Intl.Segmenter primary + OR-condition fallback (retired OQ resolved) - **Status**: SUPERSEDED by `DR-0277`. The lower bound this record mandates is gone, and the OR it chose is not how the unit is selected. - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: `countWords` (consumed by QFAI-PROT-002) MUST use `Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "word" })` with the `isWordLike` filter as primary; when `Intl.Segmenter` is unavailable (Node < 18 or stripped runtime) the fallback is the OR-condition `200..500 words OR 600..2500 characters`. Error text on out-of-band MUST name (a) the count form actually measured, (b) the band used. Acceptance signal: Japanese 800–1500-char fixture passes, English 200–500-word fixture continues to pass. - Chosen option: Intl.Segmenter primary + OR-condition fallback. - Rejected option — heuristic-only (CJK character-ratio fallback to `len/2`): brittle on mixed-script bilingual prose; misclassifies headers / inline code / latin loanwords. - DO NOT: ship CJK-ratio heuristic as primary. Temptation: cheaper than ICU dependency. - Rejected option — OR-condition only (no semantic word resolution): loses the "200..500 words" semantic for English; tests cannot distinguish word-shaped prose from filler character counts. - DO NOT: drop semantic word measurement. Temptation: one rule covers both locales. - Rejected option — per-rule configurable bounds in `.qfai/contracts/prototyping/*.yaml`: per-project config sprawl; each consumer must hand-tune; defeats the canonical-band invariant. - DO NOT: push the band into per-project config. Temptation: maximum flexibility. - Source REQ: REQ-0106. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). #### DR-0001-0004: `certify` ↔ `verify` resolution — option B (`verify.json#scope` field) (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: Introduce a `scope: "prototyping" \| "atdd" \| "full"` field on `verify.json`. `qfai prototyping certify --check` accepts `scope: "prototyping"` as satisfying the prototyping-phase gate. `completion-certificate.json` MUST explicitly record `scope: "prototyping"` and MUST NOT claim full DONE. Full DONE retains the original full `/qfai-verify` PASS gate. Co-shipped helper `certify --upgrade-scope full` (per retired OQ=A, separate row in resolution log) allows in-place re-seal once full verify PASSes. - Chosen option: B (scope-field on verify.json). - Rejected option A — split certify into `--prototype-only` / `--full`: fragments the command surface; doubles the documentation matrix; each new scope requires a new flag. - DO NOT: add `--prototype-only` flag. Temptation: feels like the lightest implementation. - Rejected option C — new `qfai prototyping verify` subcommand: duplicates the verify command surface; each new scope adds a new subcommand. Discoverability is offset by per-scope command proliferation. - DO NOT: introduce a parallel verify command. Temptation: most-discoverable from skill body. - Source REQ: REQ-0112, REQ-0113. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). #### DR-0001-0005: Multi-spec doc/impl drift — option A (realign SKILL.md to single-spec) (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: Realign `qfai-prototyping/SKILL.md` to single-spec language. Remove `resolveSurfaceUnion()` from the public skill surface (the function remains internal, callable from validators and `show-spec`, but is no longer documented as a skill capability). The per-spec layout migration (`iter-NN/spec-NNNN/<screen-id>.{png,html,review.json}`) is deferred to a future pack; surface MUST be self-consistent at HEAD (no claim of multi-spec iterate/certify until the migration lands). - Chosen option: A (realign SKILL.md to single-spec). - Rejected option B — complete per-spec layout migration: requires iterate emit + certify consume + path helpers + validator rewrites in the same pack; not justified by current downstream demand and would inflate this remediation pack beyond an atomic slice. - DO NOT: bundle the per-spec migration into this defect-remediation pack. Temptation: "fix it once and for all" is appealing but breaks atomicity. - Source REQ: REQ-0114, REQ-0124. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). Cross-link: TDD-0384 / OQ-0012-0006 remains the future-pack tracker for option B. #### DR-0001-0006: `lap-009` / `lap-010` severity — advisory-failing (severity error + mandatory justification) (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: The new layout anti-patterns `lap-009: duplicate-capture` and `lap-010: missing-route` (introduced by REQ-0121) are severity **error** with mandatory Reviewer justification text. They interrupt convergence; the Reviewer MUST emit a non-empty `justification:` naming the duplicate md5 pair (for lap-009) or the missing route (for lap-010) to clear the gate. This mirrors the `R-*` advisory-failing pattern (DR-0258). - Chosen option: advisory-failing (error + mandatory justification). - Rejected option — advisory-warning: does not interrupt drift; identical capture pairs and missing routes would silently accumulate; defeats the md5/route detection's purpose. - DO NOT: ship lap-009/010 as warning. Temptation: mirrors lap-001..008 historical posture. - Source REQ: REQ-0121. - Trace: discussion-20260523221141355 retired OQ (deferred → resolved here). #### DR-0001-0007: Screen-id casing — option A (underscore end-to-end) (retired OQ resolved) - Date: 2026-05-24 - Statement: `screens[].id` MUST be snake_case (underscore-separated) end-to-end.
